Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in asteroids theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 525 and the invariant I(A,B) = 975 mod 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to asteroids structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 525/975 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the asteroids object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 54. The moduli space has dimension at most 35 with 2 connected components.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the asteroids decomposition. By minimality, the element a^54 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 35-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
